### Professional Summary

I am currently serving as an Assistant Professor in the PG and Research Department of Chemistry at J.K.K. Nataraja College of Arts & Science. With over six years of teaching experience and more than six years of dedicated research experience, I have established myself in the field of nanomaterials and energy storage applications. I completed my undergraduate studies in Chemistry at Erode Arts College, followed by a postgraduate degree from Bharathiar University. I earned my Ph.D. with a focus on materials science from Jeju National University. My research primarily focuses on the development of organic and inorganic nanostructured thin films for multifunctional and energy storage applications. I have made significant research contributions, particularly in the area of supercapacitors, with numerous publications in reputed Scopus-indexed journals. My work emphasizes sustainable materials, including biomass-derived activated carbon and nanocomposite electrodes. I have successfully guided Ph.D. scholars and am actively supervising ongoing research in advanced energy storage systems. I have been the recipient of several prestigious awards and fellowships, including postdoctoral fellowships, the DST-INSPIRE Faculty Fellowship, and recognitions such as the Best Young Researcher Award. In addition to teaching and research, I actively participate in academic development programs, conferences, and workshops, contributing as a resource person and organizer.
Overall, I am a dedicated educator and innovative researcher committed to advancing materials chemistry and sustainable energy technologies.

### Professional Summary

Dr. Hemalatha Devaraj is an accomplished Microbiologist and Geneticist with over 15 years of experience spanning academic leadership, industrial research, and advanced genomic sciences. Currently serving as the Head of the Department of Microbiology at J.K.K. Nataraja College of Arts and Science, she has a proven track record in departmental administration, curriculum design as a Board of Studies Chairman, and the establishment of high-end laboratory infrastructure.

Her technical expertise is rooted in metagenomics, yeast metabolic engineering, and microbial genetics, supported by a Ph.D. from Madurai Kamaraj University and prior experience as a Scientist at Evolva Biotech. A CSIR-NET qualified professional, she has authored multiple peer-reviewed publications and contributed extensively to the scientific community through research on the human oral microbiome and enzyme mutagenesis.

### Professional Summary

தமிழ்மொழி மற்றும் இலக்கியத்தில் ஆழ்ந்த ஆர்வம் கொண்ட நான், மாணவர்களின் அறிவு, சிந்தனை மற்றும் பண்பாட்டு விழிப்புணர்வை மேம்படுத்தும் வகையில் கற்பித்தலில் ஈடுபட்டு வருகிறேன். மாணவர் மையப்படுத்தப்பட்ட கற்றல் முறைகளைப் பயன்படுத்தி, பயனுள்ள கற்றல் சூழலை உருவாக்குகிறேன். மேலும், கருத்தரங்குகள் மற்றும் ஆராய்ச்சிகளில் பங்கேற்று, தொழில்முறை திறன்களை தொடர்ந்து மேம்படுத்தி வருகிறேன்.

### Professional Summary

Dr. A. Aravinth Raja is an accomplished academician and researcher currently serving as Assistant Professor of English at J.K.K. Nataraja College of Arts and Science (Autonomous). With over five years of teaching experience and more than seven years of research engagement, he has established himself as a dynamic scholar in the fields of English Studies and Linguistics. He holds dual Master’s degrees in English and Linguistics, a PGDTE from EFLU Hyderabad, and a Ph.D., and has qualified UGC-NET (English and Linguistics), TNSET, and UGC-JRF.

His research is interdisciplinary, situated at the intersection of Applied Linguistics, ESL pedagogy, Cognitive Language Studies, and Cultural Studies, with a growing emphasis on AI-mediated language learning. His scholarly contributions include 16 research publications, a book on Salman Rushdie, and presentations at 23 national and international conferences. He has also delivered over 20 invited talks and keynote addresses, reflecting his active engagement with academic communities.

Dr. Aravinth Raja’s teaching philosophy integrates technology, cultural sensitivity, and innovative pedagogical practices, particularly in multilingual and ESL contexts aligned with NEP 2020. In addition to his academic work, he has held several administrative and leadership roles, including President of the Institution’s Innovation Council and member of IQAC, contributing significantly to institutional development and academic quality assurance.

A recipient of multiple recognitions, including Best Paper Award and NPTEL Topper distinctions, he continues to contribute to research, teaching, and academic leadership with a strong commitment to excellence and innovation in higher education.

### Professional Summary

தொழில்முறை சுருக்கம்

 தமிழ்மொழி மற்றும் இலக்கியத்தின் மேம்பாட்டில் ஆழ்ந்த ஆர்வம் கொண்ட நான், கற்பித்தலின் மூலம் மாணவர்களின் அறிவாற்றல் மற்றும் பண்பாட்டு புரிதலை வளர்க்க உறுதியாக செயல்படுகிறேன்.

எனது கல்வி பின்னணியுடன், பழமையான மற்றும் நவீன தமிழ் இலக்கியம், மொழியியல் மற்றும் இலக்கிய விமர்சனம் ஆகிய துறைகளில் ஆர்வம் கொண்டுள்ளேன். மாணவர் மையப்படுத்தப்பட்ட கற்பித்தல் முறைகளை பின்பற்றி, பயனுள்ள மற்றும் ஈர்க்கக்கூடிய கற்றல் சூழலை உருவாக்க முயற்சிக்கிறேன்.

மேலும், கருத்தரங்குகள், பணிமுனைகள் மற்றும் ஆராய்ச்சி நடவடிக்கைகளில் தொடர்ந்து ஈடுபட்டு, எனது தொழில்முறை வளர்ச்சியையும் தமிழியல் வளர்ச்சியையும் முன்னேற்ற முயல்கிறேன்.

### Professional Summary

Dr. C. Bharathi is an erudite scholar and perspective critic of fiction and drama. She teaches English at J K K Nataraja College of Arts and Science, Komarapalayam, Tamil Nadu. Her areas of special interest include Indian English Fiction, Postcolonial Literature, Expatriate Writing and New Literatures on which she has presented / published several research papers. She participated and presented research papers in numerous National/International Conferences/Seminars. Her work is extensively published in reputed journals. Her published books are Multicultural Concerns: Studies in Contemporary Fiction and Diaspora Allegiance: Chitra Banerjee Divakaruni’s Fiction.

He integrates AI-driven approaches such as fashion forecasting, virtual prototyping, and personalized apparel design with traditional textile knowledge to enhance teaching, research, and student skill development. His contributions include organizing workshops, publishing research, and facilitating industry-oriented training programs supported by government initiatives.
Recognized as “Best Faculty of the Year 2023,” he is committed to promoting innovation, sustainability, and technology-enabled learning, preparing students for the evolving fashion industry driven by creativity, automation, and smart manufacturing.

### Professional Summary

தமிழ்மொழி மற்றும் இலக்கியத்தின் செழுமையைப் பேணிக் காக்கும் எண்ணத்துடன், மாணவர்களுக்கு தரமான கல்வியை வழங்கும் ஆசிரியராக நான் செயல்பட்டு வருகிறேன். கற்பித்தலை ஒரு பணியாக அல்லாது, மாணவர்களின் திறன்களை வெளிக்கொணரும் ஒரு பொறுப்பாகக் கருதி செயல்படுகிறேன்.

பழமையும் புதுமையும் இணைந்த தமிழ் இலக்கியம், மொழியியல் மற்றும் விமர்சன ஆய்வு துறைகளில் தொடர்ந்து ஆர்வம் செலுத்தி, அவற்றை மாணவர்களிடம் எளிமையாகவும் பயனுள்ளதாகவும் எடுத்துரைப்பதில் கவனம் செலுத்துகிறேன். மாணவர் மையப்படுத்தப்பட்ட மற்றும் செயல்விளைவுடைய கற்றல் முறைகள் மூலம் சிந்தனைத் திறன் வளர்ச்சியை ஊக்குவிக்கிறேன்.

மேலும், கல்வி சார்ந்த கருத்தரங்குகள், பயிற்சிகள் மற்றும் ஆராய்ச்சி முயற்சிகளில் ஈடுபட்டு, எனது அறிவையும் தொழில்முறை திறன்களையும் மேம்படுத்திக் கொண்டு, தமிழியல் வளர்ச்சிக்குப் பங்காற்ற முனைவதாக இருக்கிறேன்.

### Professional Summary

A committed academician and researcher with over 13 years of research experience in stochastic inventory systems and 10 years of teaching experience at the collegiate level.

Serves as a Programme Officer of the National Service Scheme (NSS), actively contributing to student development and community engagement initiatives. Experienced as a question paper setter for university examinations and various autonomous colleges.

Completed a three-month research internship at the Fraunhofer Technical Institute, Germany, gaining international exposure and research collaboration experience. A life member of the Indian Science Congress Association, Coimbatore.

### Professional Summary

Dr. V. Sasirekha is a distinguished academic and research professional with over two decades of experience in computer applications, research, and institutional development. She has made significant scholarly contributions with over 25 publications in national and international journals and has authored and edited books in areas including DevOps. Her research and academic expertise are complemented by extensive experience in teaching, Ph.D. supervision, and academic leadership roles, including active involvement in accreditation processes such as NBA, NAAC, and NIRF. She has guided multiple doctoral scholars and contributed to editorial and peer-review activities for reputed journals. Recognized for her impact in academia, she has received several accolades, including awards for influential women educators and distinguished scholarship. Her work reflects a strong commitment to quality education, research excellence, and institutional advancement.

### Professional Summary

தமிழ்மொழி மற்றும் இலக்கியத்தில் ஆழ்ந்த ஆர்வம் கொண்ட நான், மாணவர்களின் அறிவு, சிந்தனை மற்றும் பண்பாட்டு விழிப்புணர்வை மேம்படுத்தும் வகையில் கற்பித்தலில் ஈடுபட்டு வருகிறேன். மாணவர் மையப்படுத்தப்பட்ட கற்றல் முறைகளைப் பயன்படுத்தி, பயனுள்ள கற்றல் சூழலை உருவாக்குகிறேன். மேலும், கருத்தரங்குகள் மற்றும் ஆராய்ச்சிகளில் பங்கேற்று, தொழில்முறை திறன்களை தொடர்ந்து மேம்படுத்தி வருகிறேன்.

### Professional Summary

Dr. S. Umavathi is currently serving as the Controller of Examinations at J.K.K. Nataraja College of Arts and Science, alongside her role as Head and Assistant Professor of the Department of Zoology. A distinguished academician with an M.Sc., Ph.D., and PGDCA, she specializes in Entomology and has over a decade of rich experience in teaching, research, and academic administration. As Head of the Department, she has been instrumental in strengthening the academic and research profile of Zoology through quality teaching, curriculum development, and active research initiatives. She has published numerous research articles in reputed national and international journals and has authored several books and book chapters. She has also successfully guided postgraduate, M.Phil., and Ph.D. scholars, contributing significantly to research development. In her role as Controller of Examinations, she ensures the smooth, transparent, and efficient conduct of examinations, upholding the integrity and standards of the evaluation system. Her dedication to academic excellence and research continues to foster a vibrant and intellectually stimulating environment within the institution.

He holds a Ph.D. in Electrochemistry from Periyar University and has demonstrated academic excellence by qualifying both the NET (69th Rank) and SET exams.Core Expertise & ResearchSpecialization: Highly skilled in Organic and Physical Chemistry, with specific research expertise in Electrochemistry and Environmental Chemistry.Research Impact: Author of numerous high-impact publications in prestigious journals such as Chemosphere (Impact Factor: 8.8) and Journal of the Mechanical Behavior of Biomedical Materials.Key Research Areas:Development of polymer-impregnated nanoceramic coatings for biomedical and environmental applications.Microwave-assisted activation of carbon for dye removal and wastewater treatment.Green synthesis of carbon quantum dots and fabrication of superhydrophobic coatings.Publications: Has published multiple books and chapters through international publishers like Elsevier and Lambert, focusing on polymers in medicine and nanostructured materials.Academic Leadership & InnovationMentorship: Currently guiding doctoral research, including the supervision of Ph.D. scholars.Pedagogy: Employs diverse teaching methodologies, including lecture-cum-discussion, visual aids (audio/video), and hands-on computer training.Professional Development: Actively participates in advanced Faculty Development Programs (FDPs) and Refresher Courses at institutions such as Infosys BPO, Mysore University, and Bharathiyar University.Academic Service: Serves as a question paper setter for both UG and PG levels for multiple institutions.
